1b1(rgyud): @#//rgya gar skad du/
2a1(rgyud): @##/!/Ar many+dzu shrI sad 
2a2(rgyud): ta pre to pa de sha/gu h+ya tan tra rA dzA/byi shi 
2b1(rgyud): ka ti ka kro d+ha bi dza yA/an dza na shi ba kan/bod 
2b2(rgyud): skad du/'phags pa 'jam dpal gsang ba'i rgyud kyi 
2b3(rgyud): rgyal po//pra khog bslang ba'i man ngag/phyi mi' rgyal 
3a1(rgyud): @#//po bsrung ba'i lung /khro bo 
3a2(rgyud): rnam par rgyal ba'i rgyud phyi ma'i yang phyi ma 'di/le'u
3a3(rgyud): nyi shu rtsa gnyis pa/nga ni dus gsum sngon du byung //
3b1(rgyud): nga ni dus gsum phyis kyang 'byung //nga ni dus gsum da lta 
3b2(rgyud): byung //nga ni lnga brgya tha ma'i tshe//nga'i bstan pa 'byung bar byed//
3b3(rgyud): bstan pa thams cad nub pa'i tshe//nga'i bstan pa 'byang bar byed//
3b4(rgyud): las ngan 'tshams med byed pa'i tshe//de tshe nga'i bstan pa 'byung //
4a1(rgyud): @#//zas ngan lci ba za ba'i tshe//de tshe nga'i bstan 
4a2(rgyud): pa 'byung //gos ngan dug hrul gon pa'i tshe//de tshe nga'i bstan pa 'byung //
4a3(rgyud): nga ni dus gsum 'byung ba'i sku//bstan pa thams cad nub pa'i tshe//de tshe 
4a4(rgyud): nga'i bstan pa 'byung //ji lta bur ni zhi 'gyur yang //nga ni thams cad zhi 
4b1(rgyud): ba'i sku//ji lta bur ni khros gyur pa//nga ni thams cad khros pa'i sku//lha rnams thams 
4b2(rgyud): cad khyed nyon cig/bcom ldan 'das 'jam pa'i dbyangs la phyag 'tshal lo//de nas 
4b3(rgyud): 'jam pa'i dbyangs kyi ni//khro bo'i ming ni gshin rje gshed//mtshon cha me lce 'bar bas 
4b4(rgyud): 'khrugs//drag la mthu rtsal drag ldan pa//de mthong nas ni khro bo gzhan//thams 
4b5(rgyud): cad kyi ni lag cha shor//de bzhin du ni 'jig rten pa'i//lha rnams kun gyis 'dud par 
5a1(rgyud): @#//byed/gzhan yang thal mo sbyar byas te//skrag nas bstod pa mang po byed//lha rnams de'i gzi 
5a2(rgyud): brjid kyis//gzir bzhin du ni rab smras pa//bcom ldan bdag cag sangs rgyas dang//chos 
5a3(rgyud): dang dge 'dun skyabs mchis kyis//rab tu mi bzad 'jigs pa'i gzugs//gzi brjid 'bar 
5a4(rgyud): bas phyogs khyab pa//khams gsum 'khrugs par mdzad pa mthong //gang tshe lha dang lha min rnams//
5a5(rgyud): skrag nas skyabs su dong ba na//de tshe 'jam pa'i dbyangs kyis ni//khro bo sngar yang zhi bar 'gyur//
5b1(rgyud): gshin rje gshed ces bya ba yi//khro bo chen po zhi ba la//de nas skyabs su dong ba yi//lha rnams kun gyis zhus 
5b2(rgyud): pa ni|khyod kyi brtson 'grus mchog 'tshal bas//ji ltar bur na mnyes par bgyi//ji ltar yang ni dang bar 'gyur//
5b3(rgyud): bdag cag the tshom chen po mchis//khro bo sa lha rnams tshig gsan nas//gshin rje gshegs kyis bka' stsal pa//
5b4(rgyud): lha rnams thams cad khyed nyon cig/gzi brjid chen po khyed rnams kun//gang tshe gsum la skyabs song bas//
5b5(rgyud): de tshe nga'i mi bzad pa//khro bo yid la dran par gyis//dkon mchog gsum ni gnas pa dang //de'i bka' yang bskyang 
5b6(rgyud): ba dang //sems can kun la snying brtse'i phyir//nga'i yid ni zhi 'gyur gyis//khyed rnams skyabs su dong ba na//
6a1(rgyud): @##//byang chub sems dang ldan pa yis//thams cad de ni grub pa'i mchog/
6a2(rgyud): yi ge 'bru ni bcu bdun po//nga'i snying po yid las byung //lha rnams kun gyis gzung bar bgyis//gshin 
6a3(rgyud): rje gshed po bdag nyid kyis//snying po rab tu gsungs pa na//de nas sa ni g.yos par 'gyur//rgya mtsho yang 
6a4(rgyud): ni rab tu khrugs//gnod sbyin dang ni srin po dang //sha za la sogs phung bar 'gyur//lha rnams kun gyis phyag 
6a5(rgyud): 'tshal zhing //yan lag kun la spu langs nas//gshin rje gshed po khro bo yi//gtso bo'i snying po de gzung ngo //
6a6(rgyud): de nas dam chos srung ba'i phyir//khro bo rnam par rgyal ba yis//rtog pa 'di ni de dag la//gshin rje gshed kyi 
6b1(rgyud): rgyas par gsungs//de nas khro bo rnam rgyal gyis//rtog 'di khro bo nyid gsungs pa//snying rje byang chub sems ldan pa'i//lha 
6b2(rgyud): rnams kyis thos dgar gyur nas//dkon gsum ni srung ba'i phyir//so so yi ni rtog pa bshad//de dag gi ni rtsa ba'i rgyud//
6b3(rgyud): khro bo nyid kyis rab gsungs so//khro bo rnam rgyal zhes bya 'di//'jig rten 'das dang 'jig rten pas//sngon ni phyag byas 
6b4(rgyud): khro bo yi//zhal nas ji bzhin thos nas kyang //khro bo nyid kyis gsungs pa yi//rgyud 'di byang chub sems rnams kyis//
6b5(rgyud): snying rje de bzhin rgyas pa dang //rnam pa mang por byin gyis brlabs//'jam dpal gyi rtsa ba'i rtog pa rnam par rgyal ba las//
6b6(rgyud): rgyud byung ba zhes bya ba'i skabs te le'u dang po'o////de nas khro bo rnam par rgyal//'jam 
6b7(rgyud): dpal khro bos bshad pa yis//rtogs pa smadu byung ba'i mchog/mdor ni rab tu bshad par bya//de la 'di'i dkyil 'khor 
7a1(rgyud): ni/dngos grub thams cad sbyin mdzad pa//'jig rten gsum po 'khrug par mdzad//yi dgas 
7a2(rgyud): mang po zhes bya'o//thams cad du ni dkyil 'khor 'di//gnas ni gang du 'grub par 'gyur//sngags pas thog ma nyid du ni//de'i gnas 
7a3(rgyud): ni brtag par bya//shing gcig pa dang dur khrod dam//khyim stong dang ni bzhi mdo dang //mchod rten dang ni mu gnyis dang //thibs po stong 
7a4(rgyud): pa lam srang snying //skyed mo tshal dang ri'i rtse//chu bo'i 'gram dang nags tshal dang //yang na rgya mtsho'i 'gram dang ni//lha chen gyi ni 
7a5(rgyud): gnas kyang rung //ba lang lhas sam yul gyi sa//khyad par ni yul rtsa bar//gang du yid ni dga' bar yang //der ni dkyil 'khor 
7a6(rgyud): rab tu bya//de nas rigs 'dzin nan tan gyis//sa ni yang dag sbyang byas te//ngo mtshar med cing ma gas dang //mnyam zhing zug du med pa 
7a7(rgyud): la//shar dang byang ni mtho ba la//kha dog snum pa lhag par bzang //ba yi lnga dang ldan pa yi//ba'i lci bas byug par bya//ci rigs pa 
7b1(rgyud): par ni byugs nas kyang //slob dpon gyis ni khrus la byas la//bsnyung par 'dug ste mnyam gzhag nas//khro bo'i bsrung bas byin gyis brlabs//
7b2(rgyud): yi dgas phal chen zhes bya ba'i//dkyil 'khor gsang mchog nub mo bri//zar ma'i ras sam ras bal la//thig skud gsum sgril mdzes pa yi//
7b3(rgyud): gru bzhi pa dang sgo bzhi par//mkhas pas dkyil 'khor thig btab ste//thog mar shar dang byang nas ni//lho dang nub kyi bar du'o//thig skud 
7b4(rgyud): de ni phyogs mtshams nas//de nas kyang ni drang bar bya//shar dang lho yi mtshams dang ni//nub dang byang gi mtshams dag tu//thig gis rab tu 
7b5(rgyud): tshad byas te//gru bzhi por ni thig gdab po//rim pa gcig pa mdzes pa la//sgo bzhi dag gis mdzes par bya//lho phyogs dag gi sgo phye 
7b6(rgyud): la|lhag ma rnams ni bcad par bya//dkyil 'khor dang ni 'tshams pa yi//dbus su pad+ma bri bar bya//pad+ma dang ni phyogs bzhi'i sgo//the 
7b7(rgyud): tshom med par mnyam par bya//mdzes byed sgo'i ched du bya//'gram pa 'dab ma'i tshad dang mnyam//nye ba yi ni mdzes byed kyang //mdzes 
8a1(rgyud): @#/ O /byed dang ni 'dra bar bya//ro ga bzhin du dkyil 'khor te//kun tu thig ni btab nas kyang //phyi nas tshul bzhin rig+sa 
8a2(rgyud): 'dzin gyis//dur khrod thal bas bris pa ni//rtag tu shar byang la sogs pa'i//gra'i nang ni de dag tu//mkhas pas be con phyag rgya bri//phyogs kyi 
8a3(rgyud): sgor ni yi dgas bzhag/gzhan yang yi dgas lnga pa ni//pad+ma'i steng du bsam par bya//de bzhin phyi sgo'i nang dag dang //phyogs rnams de dag thams 
8a4(rgyud): cad du//mi ro mgo med yi dgas rnams//dur khrod thal bas mngar te bri//de phyir yi dgas phal chen yin//khru gang la sogs rim pa nas//khru 
8a5(rgyud): brgyad pa'i bar du de//de las lhag par yongs mi bri//rtag tu bdag nyid mchod pa ni//khru gang bar ni bri bar bya//zhi ba dang ni rgyas pa dang //mi skyod 
8a6(rgyud): grub pa khru dor bya//sems can kun ni bskrad pa dang //de bzhin zhi ba khru gsum pa//khru bzhi dbang dang dgug pa yi//las rnams kyi ni mchog tu 
8a7(rgyud): gsungs//khru lnga nang dang mi nad dang //dgra rnams med par bsal ba'i mchog/khru drug bgegs rnams thams cad dang //gdon rnams nges par bzlog 
8b1(rgyud): par byed//khru bdun pa ni lha yi rnams//dbang dang dgug pa'i las rnams byed//khru brgyad pa ni dngos grub kun//sbyin byed mchog tu shes par bya//de ltar ci rig+sa
8b2(rgyud): bris nas ni//de dag gtsang mas nye reg bya//chu'i phyir ni chu'i snod//chus ni rab tu bkang ba bzhag/de bzhin me tog don du yang //me tog snyug gu dri zhim dang //
8b3(rgyud): me tog phreng ba snod mchog gzhan//legs par dgang zhing mdzes par bya//dri'i don du tsan dan mchog/tsan dan dkar po dmar po blang //a ka ru dang sbyar ba 
8b4(rgyud): yi|gu gul bdug pa de bzhin no//mar me mar gyi me mar dang //mkhas pa snying po 'jom pa'o//drag po yi ni las dag la//mi'i tshil gyis bya bar bshad//
8b5(rgyud): gtor ma nya dang sha la sogs//sbyar thug la sogs lhag par dbul//de ltar de ni bsags byas nas//de nas rig sngags 'chang ba yis//sangs rgyas 
8b6(rgyud): kun la phyag byas te//byang chub sems la dmigs nas ni//ri mo yi ni las rnams dag/gal te bri bar ma nus na//yi dwags phal chen dbus dag tu//pad+ma 
8b7(rgyud): sngar bri gang yin pa//de'i steng du yi dgas ni//mkhas pa'i yid kyis bsam par bya//yi dwags kyi ni sngags mchog gis//bsam pa dag ni byas nas kyang //
9a1(rgyud): @#/ O /gra yi dbyug pa bzhi po yang //sngags nyid kyis ni bsam par bya//gal te 'bri bar nus na ni//sngags nyid steng du bzhag par 
9a2(rgyud): bya|sgo'i yi dwags bzhi po yang //gal te bri bar ma nus na//de bzhin yi dwags 'dzin sngags kyis//byin gyis rlobs par mi byed par gsungs//rkang 
9a3(rgyud): lag mgo bo phrag pa dang //de bzhin gzhan yang dpung pa dang //thod pa keng rus mi lpags rlon//ro yi phreng ba bsam par bya//de'i nang du rang sngags kyis//byin 
9a4(rgyud): gyis brlob tu bzhug par bya//de ltar cog bzhin bsam nas//'jug tu khro bo gshin rje gshed//sngags brjod nas ni spyan drangs nas//rang gi snying por bzhugasu 
9a5(rgyud): gsol//khro bo 'khrug pa 'jigs mdzad pa//sku ni me lce 'bar ba can//gzi brjid 'phags pa bsam pa ni//sku mdog stag zil bcan ba 'dra//zhal drug sra 
9a6(rgyud): yang kham bar 'dug/smin mkham pa mig kyang ser//spyan gsum mig dang zlum pa ste//phyag ni bcu gnyis pa'i gzugs//de bzhin yi dwags steng du bzhugs//
9a7(rgyud): zhabs drug gis ni mdzes par byas//tri shul ral gri dbyug dgra sta//sbyin pa'i phyir ni phyag brkyang ba//lcags kyu yi ni mtshon cha dag/g.yas pa'i phyag ni de bzhin bsnam+sa//
9b1(rgyud): de g.yon pa'i drug po yang //thod bcug ting tho ma ra//'dzub mos sdigs dang zhags pa bsams//dag na dbugs tho bsams par bya//mi mgo rlon pa dang skaM po dang //
9b2(rgyud): yi dwags phreng bas rnam par brgyan//stag gi lpags pas nam bza' gsol//sku yang //zhal gyi dbyibs ni du ma ste//dgra ni rgya mtsho 'khrugs 
9b3(rgyud): pa ste//kha cig 'jigs par bgrad pa'i zhal//kha cig zhal ni gdangs pa ste//ljags 
9b4(rgyud): po legs gra ni//spyan drangs mchod yon dbul bar bya//de nas dri dang sbyar ba'i chus//mchod yon cho ga bzhin phul nas//yi dgas kyi ni spyi bor yang //me tog 
9b5(rgyud): dmar po bzhag par bya//blo can gyis ni khro bo la//de nas tsan dan dmar po dbul//dri'i 'og tu me tog snams//tsan dan gyis ni gtor ma dbul//me tog me tog 
9b6(rgyud): phreng ba bsnams//me tog sngags kyi dbul bar bya//me tog sngags kyis rim bzhin du//me tog cho ga bzhin phul nas/rang gi snying po sngags kyis kyang //khro 
9b7(rgyud): bo yi ni spyi bo dang //khro bo gtso bo'i snying khar ni//snying po rnams par bgod par bya//mtshogs mar de'i tshogs ma dang //de'i gtsug tu spyi gtsug bzhag /de'i 
10a1(rgyud): @#/ O /lus la go cha dang //mtshon cha'i tshogs la mtshan bzhag ste//'di skad bstan pa 'di dag ni//me tog dag gis dgod par 
10a2(rgyud): bya//de ltar ci rigs mchod nas kyang //bdug pa rang gi sngags kyis dbul//de nas mar me rab 'bar bas//phyogs brgyad dag tu dgod par bya//'byung po 
10a3(rgyud): gtor ma khro bo la//phul nas yi dwags phal chen no//phyi rol du ni mkhas pa yis//sme gtor rnams ni sbyin par bya//khro mo'i gtso mo de phyogs te//de nas 
10a4(rgyud): yang ni bdug pa dbul//de'i phyi rol ru bcas snod//me tog dag gis bkang ste bzhag/cho g+sa khrus byas bsnyung ba yi//slob ma dag ni bos nas kyang //
10a5(rgyud): sngags kun gyis ni gzigs bskyed la//khro bo yi ni snying po yis//lag gnyis me tog byin nas ni//gos kyi gdong ni g.yog par bya//dkon mchog gsum ni dran 
10a6(rgyud): byas te//slob dpon gyis ni bzhug par bya//rang gi snying pos me tog nyid //dkyil 'khor la ni dor du bzhug/de nas dbyug pa zhes bya yi//phyag rgya sngags 
10a7(rgyud): pas de la bstan//dkon mchog gsum ni gsang ba yis//dam tshig de la sbyin par bya//khro bo'i gtso bo'i sngags kyis ni//de nas sngags pas dbang bskur 
10b1(rgyud): ba|chu dang me tog dag gis kyang //'grub phyir de la sbyin par bya//de nas khro bo snying po yi//cho ga bzhin du dbang bskur nas//me tog bdug pa phreng ldan par//
10b2(rgyud): snying po de la sbyin par bya//khro bo yi ni snying po mchog/des kyang de ltar gnos nas ni//de nas bla ma phyag byas te//sngags pas bla ma mchod bya na//ci nus sbyin 
10b3(rgyud): pas mchod par bya//sngags pas bla ma mchodabyas nas//dngos grub thob 'gyur gzhan du min//slob ma snying po byin nas kyang //phreng ba tsan dan la sogs pas//
10b4(rgyud): mchod nas de'i 'og tu ni//phyi rol tu ni sngar bzhag pa'i//dri chu la sogs bkang ba'i snod//de blangs mchod yon phul nas ni//'jam dbyangs khro bo mi zad 
10b5(rgyud): pa//mthu rtsal drag po slar yang btang //btang nas de'i 'og tu ni//dkyil 'khor du yang mchod yon dbul//rgya chen sa'i dkyil 'khor ni//dngos grub 
10b6(rgyud): mtshungs med 'dod pa yis//rigs sngags kyi ni de ltar ni//mchod pa rtag tu legs par bya//'jam dpal gyi rtsa ba'i rtog pa las khro bo rnam par rgyal ba'i rgyud chen 
10b7(rgyud): po yi dwags phal po che'i dkyil 'khor gyi skabs te gnyis pa'o////de nas gzhan yang sngags rnams 'byung ste//A+oM na ma sarba ta thA 
11a1(rgyud): @#/ O /ga te bya/pre twa/mu khA/kha ta ka swAhA//dbus kyi yi dwags kyi'o//oM na ma sarba ta thA ga te b+h+ya/kro ta bi dza ya 
11a2(rgyud): a nir dzA li ta dzi he pre ta shi ra hUM phaT/'di ni gra bzhi be con bzhi'i sngags so//A+oM pre tol lka ya hUM phaT/shar sgo'i yi dwags kyi'o//A+oM pre ta b+hi man 
11a3(rgyud): ta ka hUM phaT//lho sgo'i yi dwags kyi'o//A+oM pre ta dza la hUM phaT//nub sgo'i yi dwags kyi'o/A+oM k+shi prA+e tod ka phaT//byang sgo'i yi dgas kyi'o//A+oM 
11a4(rgyud): nA mA sarbA ta thA ga te bya//prA+e ta ma le bi kri te//g+hu h+ya pa tsi ka mungs rba ra k+she ba dzus Sh+To hUM phaT//yi dgas phreng ba'i 'o//de dag ni yi dwags kyi dkyil 'khor 
11a5(rgyud): gyi sngags kyi tshogs so//da ni khro bo gshin rje gshed kyi spyon dreng ba la sogs pa'i sngags rnams 'byung ngo //oM na mo bud d+ha/d+harmAs ge bya/kro ta bA rA bis 
11a6(rgyud): swa ma ye na/a gad tsha/oM A hUM prid dam pe ta a sa nan/pre tod ka ta na rak+sha phaT swAhA//kho bo'i rgyal po gshin rje gshed spyan drangs pa'i sngags so//oM a 
11a7(rgyud): kro te ka ya man tA ka hA ma tha ban dza hUM phaT/'di ni khro bo'i rgyal po rang gi rtog pa yin pas de'i sngags yi ge bcu bdun pa'o//A+oM A hUM//de nyid kyi snying po'o//oM 
11b1(rgyud): ka la ru pa hUM phaT//tshogs ma'i 'o//A+oM ya man ta ka ye ka bI rA hUM phaT//spyi gtsug gi'o//oM dza la mi ta bi gra ha hUM phaT/go cha'o//A+oM bA+e bas swAhA/na ta ka hUM phaT/
11b2(rgyud): mtshon cha'i 'o//'di ni khro bo'i rgyal po gshin rje gshed kyi yan lag lnga pa'o//oM a ka hUM ya ma ta b+hi sa ra swAhA//arga'i sngags so//A+oM ta ka tsi ti sho ni 
11b3(rgyud): dar ta man gan da ba grang ha swAhA//dri'i sngags so//A+oM dib bya srag ma l+ya da ra ni swAhA//me tog gi sngags so//A+oM tsi ti pu pa pri ya yA swA hA//bdug pa'i 
11b4(rgyud): sngags so//A+oM di pa dzo ti she ha swAhA//mar me'i sngags so//A+oM bA man ta ka te tso pa li ni swAhA//lha bshos kyi sngags so//A+oM sarba A mri sha ba ba ha ri ni swAhA//
11b5(rgyud): sme gtor thams cad kyi sngags so//A+oM dib ba nan b+h+yo tsi ni ha ra ha ra d+h+ri d+h+ri d+b+hi kha swAhA//gtor ma thams cad dbul ba'i sngags so//A+oM khu khu ri phre tod kha re pre ta ma 
11b6(rgyud): le la li swAhA//m+lAM m+lAM gleng da/pri pri d+ho shi b+he//d+h+ring d+h+ring d+h+ring khu khu ri kri han ba ling swAhA//yi dwags mang po'i mang po'i dkyil 'khor gyi gtor ma'i sngagaso//
11b7(rgyud): khro bo rnam par rgyal ba'i rtsa ba'i rtog pa las/yi dgas mang po'i gtor ma'i sngags te le'u gsum pa'o////de nas gzhan yang 'jam dbyangs 
12a1(rgyud): @#/ O /kyis//khro bo dpa' bo gcig pu ni//tshad med gzi brjid bdag nyid che//de'i phyag rgya bshad par bya'//lag pa g.yas pa 
12a2(rgyud): 'dun du byas//dkon mchog gsum gyi dam tshig go/mngon par bstod pa byed pa yi//phyag rgya cho ga bzhin du bcings//rigs 'dzin dag ni rtag par ni//lag 
12a3(rgyud): pa'i sor mo'i thams cad kyis//mthe bong legs par sbas nas ni//rab tu bsdams pa bzang ba bcing //cho ga shes pas be con rgya//snying po sngags 
12a4(rgyud): sam logs shig tu//sngags pas phyag rgya'i sngags kyis bcing //gzhan du nam kyang mi bcings (  )ngo //A+oM na mo bud ta d+har ma sang ge bya/a sarba ta thA ga ta/
12a5(rgyud): kro ta bi bi dza ya a ga nir dza li ta/dz+wa ha pre ta shri hUM phaT/be con gyi phyag rgya'i gsang sngags so//phyag rgya cig pur gyur pa 'di//khro bo'i snying por sngags 
12a6(rgyud): par byed//mtshon cha ma lus pa'i gtso//bdud rnams thams cad zhi bar byed//ji ltar dkyil 'khor du bri ba//de'i cho ga bshad par bya//be con 
12a7(rgyud): dbyug pa lta bu'i tshul//'di ni be con zhes par bshad //khyad par du ni de'i rtser//yi dwags mgo bo mkhas pas bri//yi dwags mang po rtag tu bri//de las 
12b1(rgyud): gzhan du nam kyang min//rigs 'dzin gyis ni cho ga bzhin//rgya 'di dur khrod thal bas so//yang na 'di nyid log cig tu//gang gi tshe na bsgrub 'dod pas//
12b2(rgyud): bsgrub pos dngos grub 'dod pa yi//rang gi dkyil 'khor du bri'o//de nas phyag rgya bsgrub pa yi//dkyil 'khor 'di ni bshad par bya//gru bzhi snga ma 
12b3(rgyud): bzhin du ste//yi dwags phal chen 'dir yang bri//de'i dbus su bi con bzhag/phyogs brgyad rnams su 'ang de bzhin no//phyag rgya'i rku dang ldan pa yi//phyag 
12b4(rgyud): rgya'i dkyil 'khor zhes bya'o//de bzhin du ni lho phyogs sgo//pyes te de nas mchod par bya//phyag rgya'i sngags ni ma lus pa'o//khro bo rgyal po'i bar du 
12b5(rgyud): bzhag/de'i sa 'dir mchod 'gyur bas//sangs ba'i A+oM//A+oM na mo bud d+harma sang ge bya//a a sarba ta thA ga ta kro ta bi dza ya//ar nir dza li ta/dzi ha 
12b6(rgyud): pre ta shi ri hUM phaT hUM/'di ni khro bo 'i rgyal po'i bar du bcug pa'i be con gyi phyag rgya'i gsang sngags so//'di las thams cad bsgrub par bya'o//de nas 
12b7(rgyud): dngos grub kun gyi mchog/khro bo'i gtso bo de kho na//gsang ba'i mchod pa de ldan pa'i//be con bsgrub par bshad par bya//snying po'i ze ba mdzes pa yi//
13a1(rgyud): @#/ O /pad+ma 'dab ma brgyad byas la//pad+ma'i rtse mor phyogs brgyad du//be con khro bo'i bar bcu bri//dbus su dgu pa bzhag nas ni//gzhan 
13a2(rgyud): rnams shar phyogs nas brtsams te/bskor ba'i tshul du go rims bzhin//me tog dag gis bzhag par bya//be con kun gyi rtse mor hUM//yi dwags sbyi bor bzhag
13a3(rgyud): par bya//thog mar A+oM dang ldan bya'i ta//A+oM na ma mched par 'gyur //a 'di khro bo rnam rgyal gyi//sa bon dam tshig yin par bshad//oM na ma bud d+ha d+harma sang 
13a4(rgyud): ge bya a//sangs rgyas kun gyis mngon par bstod //be con rnams ni thams cad kyang //rang gi tshig gis bskul bar ldan//thams cad rang gi dam tshig ldan//
13a5(rgyud): de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs las byung //bskul ba'i tshig dang rab ldan zhing //khro bo'i lta bas brtags pa dang //rang rang sku 'dzin bskul nas ni//de ma 
13a6(rgyud): thag tu las byed 'gyur//yang na khro bo rnam rgyal gyi//khro bo'i lha bas brtag pa'o//rang gi dam tshig dang ldan pa//hUM dang ldan pas bskul ba bzhag/zhi ba'i 
13a7(rgyud): rgyas pa dbang dang dgag /dbye ba dang ni bskud pa rnams//gsang ba'i mchod pa sbyor ba yis//bdag gis mdor ni bshad par bya//de'i thog mar yi ge A+oM//
13b1(rgyud): de las gzhan pa sngags 'phel byed//de bzhin gshegs pa'i dam tshig nyid//gsum pa yin par yongs su bsgrags//sngags kyi skul ba'i dam tshig 'dir//dngos
13b2(rgyud): grub ster ba bzhi pa ste//'di ni sngags la skul ba'i tshig/sarba ta thA ga ta zhes//brjod par sngags kyi bdag nyid do//'jug gi hUM ni khro bo yi//sngags ni 'phel
13b3(rgyud): bar byed par bshad//de nas gzhan gyi khyad par du//rang gi bskul ba'i tshig gis byed//'di dbang bya bar 'dod pas ni//rang gi ngo bo smos par bya//lha ni dbang
13b4(rgyud): du byos shig ces//brjod nas tha ma swA hA sbyar//zhi ba'i phyir ni bskul ba nyid//mjug tu bA+ai shad brtag par bya//de bzhin gshegs pa'i rig las byung //mtshungs
13b5(rgyud): pa med pa'i dpal 'dod pas//bskul ba'i tshig gi 'jug tu ni//na ma swA hA rtag tu sbyar//rgyas pa'i phyir ni sngon bzhin du//bskul nas pe bshad sbyar bar bya//
13b6(rgyud): rgyas phyir bskul ba'i thog ma dang //khro bo'i hUM ni tha mar sbyar//gsad pa la ni phaT ces bya//'bar ba 'di la lhag pa yin//rigs rnams 'jig par bya ba la//
13b7(rgyud): hUM dbus khra dang ldan pa'i phaT//de bzhin bskrad la bskul ba yi//mgo dang mjug tu hUM bzhag go/yang dag 'bar ba'i phaT kyang bzhag/gsang ba'i mchod pa'i cho
14a1(rgyud): gar grags//de ltar mchod pa'i cho ga ste//be con dgus ni byas par 'gyur//gsang ba'i mchod pa sbyor ba yis//
14a2(rgyud): bdug pa me tog de bzhin bri//mar me gtor ma de bzhin te//bzlas pa med kyang 'grub par 'gyur//gsang ba'i chod pa'i sbyor ba yis//dbang du bya dang byed pa dag/
14a3(rgyud): bskrad dgug la sogs pa yi ni//ji skad gsungs ba'i tshul dag dang //chos grangs dag kyang skrag pa dang //bsnyung ba dag kyang bya mi dgos//gsang ba'i mchod pa cho ga 
14a4(rgyud): shes//phyag rgya'i dkyil 'khor cho ga byas//khrus sam yang na khrus ma byas//ji ltar 'dod pa'i rang dgar spyod//ci yang rung ba'i gtor ma dang //lo ma me tog 'bras 
14a5(rgyud): bus kyang //gang gis gshin rje gshed po yis//snying po phyag rgyas mchod byed pa//byang chub sems dang ldan gyur cig/rab tu 'grub pas zla drug gis//de ni ji 
14a6(rgyud): 'dod dgos par rnyed/ji ltar 'dod pa'i las byed//'jam dpal gyi rtog pa khro bo rnam par rgyal ba las//be con phyag rgya'i dkyil 'khor du gsang ba'i mchod par 
14a7(rgyud): rtogs pa ste le'u bzhi pa'o////de nas be con phyag rgya yis//ji skad gsungs pa de dag sgrubs//de'i dkyil 'khor gnyis pa gzhan//
14b1(rgyud): dkyil na yi dgas shes bya ba//sgrub pos mtshan mo dur khrod du//dur khrod dag gis thal ba yis//gru bzhi pa la sgo bzhir bri//mi ro ro smad dag dang ldan//bla 
14b2(rgyud): re rnga yab me long dril//me tog phreng bas mdzes par byas//yi dgas mang po dang //thod pa brgyad kyis rnam par spras//phyis ni be con phyag rgya yang //mgo med 
14b3(rgyud): ro'i steng du bri//dkyil na yi dwags zhes bya ba'i//thams cad 'grub byed dkyil 'khor 'di//khro bo'i gtso bo'i phyag rgya yis//mchod pa ji skad gsung pa bya//smre 
14b4(rgyud): gtor thams cad byin nas ni//gtsang mas go cha bgos nas kyang //'bum phrag gcig ni sngar bsnyen pa//byas nas 'di ni brtsam par bya//phyugs chen dag gi khrag gis 
14b5(rgyud): kyang //shis pa'i thod pa bkang nas ni//de bzhin mi las byung ba'i sha/phyag rgya sa yis ni dbul bar bya//phyugs chen gyi ni tshil bu las//mar me 'bar 
14b6(rgyud): ba byas pa dag/de'i phyi rol phyogs brgyad du//sngags kyi bsrungs te dbul bar bya//cho ga shes pas de lta bur//byas nas sgrub la 'jug par bya//dpal 
14b7(rgyud): ldan gos ni dmar po gon//tsan dan dmar pos bkus nas ni//cung zad dag kyang mi byed par//mar gyi brgyad la gcig pus so//dpal la sems drag 
15a1(rgyud): mthu ldan pa'i//grogs ni gsum dang lhan cig tu//blo dang ldan pas sems drag cong //dbang po bsdams pa'i bsgrub pa po//
15a2(rgyud): yi dwags kyi ni dkyil 'khor du//bsgrub pa la ni 'jug par bya//de nas mtshan mo phyag rgyas bcings//brtan par 'jug la legs par bzlas//bgegs 
15a3(rgyud): rnams sna tshogs mtshan cig tu//byunge ste de las rgyal bar bya'i//de ltar srin po'i tshogs chen las//rgyal nas phyag rgya 'grub par 'gyur//mi 'grub pa'i las 
15a4(rgyud): rnams kyang //de nas thogs ma med par byed//'jam pa'i dbyangs kyi khro bo yis//bskul nas 'dir ni thogs pa med//ha na bi sba ta ban dza zha ban dza zhes bskul 
15a5(rgyud): ba'i tha mar hUM brjod cing //g.yon brkyang pa'i tshul gnas pas//khros pas ri ts+tsha bgrong dka' dang //ra ba yang thog (sgo )khang dang //pu shu khang pa mngar ba dag/skad 
15a6(rgyud): cig dag gis rnyil bar byed//da ra bi spo ta ban dza ban dza zhes pas bskul ba'i 'jug tu hUM brjod cing /ri bo dag ni mang po dang //de bzhin lha min sgo dag kyang //
15a7(rgyud): lha man sga dag kyang //de bzhin gzhan dang sa dag kyang //ji ltar 'dod pa 'gags par byed//pa rtsa b+hing traM sa sho sha zhes//bskul nas tha mar hUM brjod na//
15b1(rgyud): phyag rgya 'di yis chu rnams ni//thogs pa med par skyems par byed//mtsho chen dang ni chu bo dang //rgya mtsho dag kyang skyems par byed//stam pa ya zhes lan gnyis
15b2(rgyud): brjod//tha mar hUM bzhag de nas mu//de nas de ni 'jug tu hUM//sA+e bya ste ma bral zhes sbyar na//mi ro la ni gnas pa bzhin//nges par myur du gnas par 'gyur//
15b3(rgyud): tha mar hUM bzhag de na sa hri//k+sho pa ya zhes lan gnyis brjod//de yi tha mar yi ge ma//mkhas pa dag gis sbyar na ni//dpung ni khrugs par gyur pa la//khros
15b4(rgyud): nas ngo mtshar ci zhig che//tha mar hUM dang ga bzhag la//de nas tiSh+Ta tiSh+Ta bzhag/sngags pas gal te sbyar na ni//cho ga 'di yis glang po gnon//
15b5(rgyud): ha na bI tang sa ma ra ya//bskul nas tha mar hUM brjod cing //de nas tha mar hUM brjod//dpung dag rab tu 'chi bar 'gyur//kha kha kha hi kha hi zhes
15b6(rgyud): bskul nas//de'i tha mar hUM brjod na//dpung rnams phan tshun khrugs par ni//thogs pa med par rab tu 'grub//tha mar hUM dang de bzhin phaT//d+h+ri d+h+ri dza dza
15b7(rgyud): tring tring dag/nges pa dag tu sbyar nas ni//sngags pa khro dang ldan pa yi//khrag gi char 'phabs byed par 'gyur//tha mar hUM brjod ha ha ha//bai ta phaT ces
16a1(rgyud): @#/ O /bdag gis bya//sngags pa dpal dang ldan pa yis//bskul nas myur du rmongs par 'gyur//tha mar hUM brjod ha ha ha//de bzhin An 
16a2(rgyud): da ya dang phaT//bskul ba 'di dag dang ldan na//'gro ba dag ni ldongs par byed//tha mar hUM brjo+ed de nas hUM//de bzhin tring tring dza dza bzhag/tha mar tring dang paT 
16a3(rgyud): ldan pa//'gro ba de ma thag tu 'bebs//khro bo chen po 'di yis ni//phyag rgya de ltar 'grub 'gyur na//de tshe las rnams thams cad ni//yi dgas bskul

16a7(rgyud): A+oM bzhag la//A+oM zhes bya ba gzhan yang bzhag/a zhes bya ba gnyis pa 'o//de'i tshig gi 'jug tu hUM//bzhag ste de nas tshigs rnams kun//de ltar kro 
16b1(rgyud): ta bi dza ag nir//tshig rnams gzhan ni sbyar bar bya//de nas de yi tha mar hUM//de bzhin tshig gi thog mar A+oM//mtha' mar a bzhag de'i mtha'//hUM deng si ti 
16b2(rgyud): lan gnyis bsgom//pra pud ta zhes lan gnyis brjod//de nas de bzhin gshegs pa yi//bka' stsal nas ni phyag rgya de'i//bdag nyid kyi ni gzer zhing

16b4(rgyud): bya//khro bo'i gtso bo'i de'i ni//bka' bzhin byed de gzhan du min//gang gis sngon du bsnyan pa byas//de'i bskul ba'i cho ga 'di//ji skad gsungs pa'i las 
16b5(rgyud): rnams la//ma grub na ni nges par bya//gzhan du sngags ni gzir byas nas//bdag nyid nges par 'chi bar 'gyur//khyad par du ni dpa' bo yang //khro bo'i gtso

16b7(rgyud): 'jig rten 'das pa dang //de dag kun gyi lus kyang 'chad//phyag rgya 'di ni gzir na ni//dpag tshad bdun gyi dkyil 'khor na//gzhan gyi rnam pa gang yod 
17a1(rgyud): @#/ O /pa//dgra rnams dregs pa med par byed//'byung po skrag par byed pa dang //de bzhin lha rnams 'khrugs par byed//khro bo rab

18a1(rgyud): par du ni phyogs kun du//mkhas pa    me ni 'bar bar bya//khru brgyad lhag chad med par ni//cho ga bzhin du bri bar bya//sgo sgo dag

20a1(rgyud): @#/ O /bzlas par bya//de nyid kyis ni dbang bskur nas//ji ltar myur bar gang du rung //khro bo mchog gi bu don dbang //dkyil 'khor cho
